## Verdict

This is the strongest, most honest, and most executable money-ready plan the repo has produced. It correctly diagnoses the prior failure (broken measurement + calendar-time decision loop), prescribes the right structural fix (replay velocity for discovery + forward-lane confirmation only for promotion + explicit anti-circling rules), and is already partially self-hosting: the June-11 strategy_bt artifacts, the money_maker_ready daily report, and master_loop_inputs.json prove the MEASURE / ACT / RATCHET steps are running on the exact cadence described.

**Core strength:** brutal transparency (0 classes pass, open incidents listed same-day, portfolio math defects called out in the addendums sourced from the inputs json, do-not-relitigate list explicit).

**Core weakness (still):** the "self-correcting" properties live mostly in prose and opt-in sidecars. The highest-leverage missing piece is a single executable admission/preflight gate that actually refuses to run or publish when H1 is red, pre-registration is absent, or a family is closed. Until that exists, the loop can still circle under a better story.

No class meets Tier-2 (verified on live canonical surfaces). The velocity engine is delivering large-n replay for crypto (n=500–970 range in today's bt jsons). COMMODITY is the only focus class showing PF>1 on the honest intrabar surface, but still gated by n.

## Detailed Findings

### P0 — Enforcement is still prose + opt-in (pre-registration, H1 halt, FDR)

- Plan repeatedly states hard rules: pre-register before backtest (M-107), "any structural H1 artifact = HALT", family closes after registered comparisons, 3-null rotation, independent re-derivation.
- Reality: `tools/preregistration_verifier.py` exists and is described by prior review as "OPT-IN SIDECAR" with no production caller in the main replay path. Registry has 0 entries. Some bt (handoff_v2) voluntarily carry the key; others do not appear to.
- Same-day money_maker report lists open P1 measurement incidents (#130 exit-price writer, #131 NULL-pnl regrowth to 737 rows, #132 WF stale) while the plan header calls the 0/9 state "TRUSTWORTHY".
- Impact: the loop can still produce nice-looking weekly scorecards and "PARTIAL / probation" language without the preconditions that were supposed to make it self-correcting.

**Fix:** one mandatory preflight (e.g. `tools/loop_preflight.py --class X --require h1,prereg,fdr,forward_n_bar`) wired into the drivers and the ratchet. Fail closed. Make the existing verifier the implementation, not a sidecar.

### P1 — Velocity principle is working for discovery in rich classes; over-stated for thin focus classes + promotion

- Crypto is delivering (n=536–970 same-day). COMMODITY (current focus #2) is still thin (bt n=35, intrabar n=90).
- Plan correctly limits to 2-3 focus slots and keeps promotion on forward lane only.
- Missing: explicit per-class replay-readiness metrics (max achievable honest n this week, symbol coverage with full bar history, regime stability notes).

### P1 — Gate numbers are close but not unified (Tier-2 vs velocity gate vs success def vs weekly filter)

- Plan §0: Tier-2 PF≥1.5 / WR≥50 / n≥100 honest.
- §2 velocity gate: 95% CI LB PF >1.15 @ n≥80 forward + conc<35%.
- §10 success: ≥2 classes with honest n≥100 AND 95% CI LB >1.15 in FORWARD by 2026-09-11.
- Today's money_maker daily + weekly_filter references add more (3 months, multi-source, EXPIRED-inclusive, etc.).
- Risk: future agents cherry-pick the friendliest bar. Unify into named states (research-replay / shadow-forward / probation / money-ready / sizing-eligible) with one table.

### Portfolio mathematics (Section 5 + ADDENDUM A) — correctly self-audited, not yet in the weekly loop

- `master_loop_inputs_2026-06-11.json` (the exact source the plan cites) contains the detailed P0 (direction-blind SHORT NAV marking on 282 live positions, sign-flip corrupting daily returns/DD/breaker/sizing since 2026-05-29), sharpe_30d mislabel, CAGR snapshot-count vs calendar, MTD proxy, missing attribution, zero-cost books, etc.
- The plan openly marks portfolio figures "UNVALIDATED" until the module lands. Good.
- Gap: no PORTFOLIO lane in the §4 weekly cadence yet. It can be deprioritized every week while replay feels more urgent.

### Class universe naming (9 vs 10)

- Plan: 0/9. Live verdict json: 10 classes + CHEAP_STOCKS / PENNY / MEME variants. Weekly filter report uses 9 rows (PENNY in, CHEAP_STOCKS out per prior review).
- Minor coordination hazard. Pin the exact active verdict taxonomy in the next edition.

### Do-not-relitigate list is valuable but not (yet) machine-checkable

- Plan §8 has a concrete list. Registry exists but empty. No automated cross-check in the replay path yet (CODEX made the same point).

## Answers to the Plan's External-Review Questions (§9)

**1. Which loop step will fail first in practice and why?**

RATCHET (and the promotion/forward-emission decisions inside FORWARD). 

ACT is producing real artifacts (the 2026-06-11 bt jsons + handoff v2 with pre_registration metadata are excellent). The daily money_maker_ready report is being written. But nothing in the current tooling refuses to ratchet or emit a "probation / shadow / re-test ~Jul-9" claim when (a) H1 incidents #130/131/132 are still open, (b) the hypothesis_registry is empty for the family, or (c) the forward lane n bar is not met. The loop can look productive while the anti-circling rules remain advisory.

**2. What is the single highest-leverage missing piece?**

A single, mandatory, **executable admission / preflight controller** that sits in front of every replay batch and every promotion decision. It must combine at minimum:
- H1 health query (open P0/P1 measurement incidents from the incidents table or equivalent, age <X days)
- M-107 registration present + hash match in hypothesis_registry + falsification bar recorded
- Family ID + closed-family check (FDR accounting)
- Forward-lane n / concentration / CI bar check for any promotion claim

Existing pieces (preregistration_verifier.py, build_intrabar_truth, the incident process, entry_conditions_forward) should feed it. Until this is wired (and the ratchet script calls it and fails the scorecard on red), the plan is a high-quality spec, not a closed control system.

**3. Does the velocity principle (replay-n over live-n) hold statistically for THIS data, or does the 6-week resolution era invalidate it?**

It holds for **discovery and candidate generation** in classes with deep bar history (CRYPTO: 315 symbols, 180d×1h, delivering n=500–970 same-day on the documented harness). The intrabar re-resolution + entry-anchored + net-of-costs methodology (visible in the 06-11 bt files and the reresolve tools) is the correct adaptation to the new honest measurement era.

It does **not** yet hold for promotion or sizing. The plan is correct to keep promotion strictly on the forward lane at pre-registered bars (n≈80–150, CI LB, time-split stability). The 6-week honest-resolution window means old replay numbers need the anchoring the current harness applies; forward confirmation is still the scarce, trustworthy signal. A quantified replay → forward calibration study (rank correlation + placebo controls) is the missing measurement that would let us put a number on "how much we can trust the velocity engine."

**4. Propose one concrete experiment we have not run.**

**Replay-to-forward calibration with regime stratification (pre-registered, placebo-controlled).**

- Pre-register (M-107) under a new family: the current focus-class lead candidates (e.g. crypto_eu_us_handoff LONG-only variant, crypto_rsi5070_us) + 3 matched placebos per class (randomized entry times or label-shuffled on the same symbols/dates).
- Use the exact harness from the 2026-06-11 bt artifacts (entry-anchored first-touch, SL-wins-ties, pre-entry features, per-symbol-day dedup, net costs).
- Evaluate only on post-2026-06-10 forward entries (use entry_conditions_forward + live intrabar resolutions).
- Stratify both the replay training window and the forward test window by a simple regime variable (e.g. 30d realized-vol quartiles for the class, or BTC-beta buckets for crypto).
- Primary success metrics (pre-registered): (a) Spearman rank correlation between (replay PF + 95% CI LB) and (realized forward PF) at 30d and 60d horizons; (b) top-quartile replay candidates beat their matched placebos in forward PF in ≥2/3 of folds after FDR; (c) no material degradation in the high-vol regime bucket.
- Output: a short `reports/replay_forward_calibration_YYYY-MM.md` that either strengthens or qualifies the velocity claim in the next edition of this plan, plus a machine-readable summary the preflight gate can eventually consume.

This is cheap (replay already runs in minutes), directly addresses the "does replay predict forward under the new measurement regime?" open item, and gives the anti-circling rules a statistical basis instead of a calendar one.

The June execution artifacts show real progress on the measurement layer (intrabar, re-resolves, entry lane, same-day honest bt sweeps) and honest reporting of the remaining gaps (open #13x incidents, stale WF, empty registry, portfolio math not yet in the loop).

- Unify the promotion / money-ready / sizing gates into one named-state table.

- Execute (or at least pre-register + start) the calibration experiment in #4 and publish the numbers.

If after two more editions the CI lower bound on the focus classes has not improved under the forward lane (or the calibration study shows replay has near-zero predictive power for forward), the meta-ratchet in Section 10 should trigger: the loop itself gets a structural redesign, not just another batch of variants.

## Quant Deep Dive: Solid Proofs for Claims (Hedge Fund / Quant Team Analysis) — Pass 2 of 4h Loop

**Team lens (Grok 4.3 lead + simulated HF quant/dev team):** We treated this as an institutional performance autopsy. Goal: prove (or disprove) the master plan's core diagnoses and our prior review claims with hard numbers, code traces, DB-derived surfaces (policy_clean / intrabar), and statistical tests. Focus on user's examples: "failing due to horrible strategies?", "strategies WAY worse than a coin flip?"

We used only canonical honest surfaces (intrabar_truth_by_class.json, money_ready_verdict.json, pf_registry policy-clean, strategy_bt_*_2026-06-11.json, money_maker_ready_20260611.md) + targeted code/grep + schema context. No raw dashboard tiles. All stats re-derived live in this pass. Systematic debugging Phase 1 complete before any "fix" language.

### 1. Aggregate "Strategies" Are Reliably Worse Than Coin Flip (Statistical Proof, Not Luck)

From `audit_dashboard/data/intrabar_truth_by_class.json` (2026-06-10, the honest post-intrabar ledger the plan calls canonical):

- CRYPTO: n=1154 resolved, wr_pct=32.4% (observed wins ≈373)
- Under fair-coin null (p=0.5): expected wins = 577, std = √(n×0.25) ≈ 17.0
- z-score = (373 - 577) / 17 ≈ **-12.0 sigma**
- Two-tailed normal p-value << 10^{-16} (for practical purposes, probability 0 under randomness)

**Interpretation (HF quant view):** This is not "bad luck," "small sample," or "volatile asset class." For n>1000, a 32.4% WR is decisive evidence the *portfolio of emitted strategies* feeding CRYPTO has negative expectancy after honest entry-anchored resolution + costs. Same pattern in EQUITY (34.6% WR, n=107, also FAIL). Even the "velocity sweep" candidates (the 80-design / handoff work the plan cites as proof-of-concept) show:

- `reports/strategy_bt_crypto_2026-06-11.json` (n=970): wr_pct=37.42%, pf_net=1.1755 (sub-50% WR, only marginal PF after 16bp RT costs; h1/h2 splits both ~37% WR)

### Code Autopsy: Bollinger Squeeze Strategy Def (Exact Flaws)
From `alpha_engine/challenge_v2.py:150` (strat_bollinger_squeeze, "volatility expansion play"):

```python
def strat_bollinger_squeeze(df, sym, meta):
    """Bollinger Band squeeze breakout -- volatility expansion play."""
    close = df["Close"]
    if len(close) < 25: return None
    sma20 = close.rolling(20).mean()
    std20 = close.rolling(20).std()
    upper = sma20 + 2 * std20
    lower = sma20 - 2 * std20
    bandwidth = (upper - lower) / sma20
    price = float(close.iloc[-1])
    atr = compute_atr(df, 14)
    # Squeeze: bandwidth is at 20-bar low, then breakout
    bw_now = float(bandwidth.iloc[-1])
    bw_min = float(bandwidth.iloc[-20:].min())
    if bw_now <= bw_min * 1.05:  # Currently in squeeze
        # Check breakout direction
        if price > float(upper.iloc[-1]):
            tp = price + atr * 2.0
            sl = price - atr * 1.0
            return make_pick(sym, meta, "BUY", price, tp, sl, atr, "bb_squeeze_breakout",
                             f"BB squeeze breakout UP, BW={bw_now:.4f}")
        elif price < float(lower.iloc[-1]):
            tp = price - atr * 2.0
            sl = price + atr * 1.0
            return make_pick(sym, meta, "SELL", price, tp, sl, atr, "bb_squeeze_breakout",
                             f"BB squeeze breakout DOWN, BW={bw_now:.4f}")
    return None
```

- **Flaws (HF quant view):** Simple TA (BB squeeze + breakout), no transaction costs/spreads/slippage in signal (plan requires net-of-16bp-RT-crypto), no regime filter beyond bandwidth low (cf. regime_router.py:725 which uses it for classification but live strats ignore), assumes "expansion play" holds in crypto (live data shows collapse). Related variants (BollingerBreakoutSignal in equity_strategy_harness.py:612, BollingerMeanReversionStrategy in mean_reversion_strategies.py:13) have similar issues (no costs, no true regime, overfit to "behavioral overreaction").

- Live proof (DB + hourly_improvement_report.json + tuner_state): bollinger_squeeze frequently -2.0 pnl_pct on UUSDT/others, drawdown_gated (loss_streak=18), demoted to LOW_CONFIDENCE. In research_forex_top10 it's a "proxy" for vol compression/expansion but "NOT implemented" as core (high-Sharpe claim but our live WR ~8-11% on decent n).

- Ties to volume_spike (previous pass): both cite "institutional"/Karpoff-style narratives but blocklist/C006/C006-rapid_fire report prove adverse selection (retail FOMO at peaks; wins +0.04% max, losses -2.5%; WR 16.7% live / PF 0.014 on 78 closed; "all 3 rehab axes blocked").

### Explicit Backups to Main Plan Claims (H1-H5, Velocity, Pre-Reg, etc.)
- **H1 (resolver/measurement error, "scoreboard lied"):** Pre-intrabar (plan §1) allowed promotion of these (contaminated WR/PF; e.g., claude_gainer_st "85% WR" on tiny n or synthetic per EAGLE/MIMO reports). Post-fix intrabar + policy_clean (pf_registry) + honest at_signal_outcomes expose 7-18% WR / -EV on n=12-252+. Exactly "23-24% WR inflation, 79% dup rate, +93,965% win, EXPIRED→WON mislabels."

- **H2 (strategies only win in backtests):** Sweep bt (reports/strategy_bt_*_2026-06-11, entry-anchored/SL-wins-ties/pre-entry/net costs) show marginal PF 1.17-1.38 on crypto handoff (n=536-970, still <50% WR in splits). Live resolved from same "horrible" emitters = PF 0.01-0.48, WR 7-18%. "Sweep bt are the 'best' we can find with replay; live/intrabar versions are still sub-breakeven."

- **Entry-selection losses, not exit-geometry (plan §1-2):** The velocity harness (plan §2, skill companion, bt files) uses "entry-anchored first-touch, SL-wins-ties, strictly-pre-entry features, per-symbol-day dedup, net of costs (16bp RT crypto)". Even with controlled/favorable exits, these entries (volume spikes at peaks, BB squeeze "expansion" in chop, momentum without regime) produce -EV. "The only conditions that beat baseline are entry-time conditions."

- **No large, easily-harvestable edge at our costs (plan §1, H3 data scarcity/H4 external mispriced):** 1,278 Bonferroni slices + 81-cell exit grid + 80 fresh designs "all converge on the null. Wins that exist are SMALL (PF 1.3-1.5 class)". Live data: 23/56 strats <35% WR (n>5), class 32-39% after honest (z=-12 sigma impossible under coin flip). Costs + adverse sel + no regime eat any narrative edge. "There is no large, easily-harvestable edge in our universe at our costs."

- **Velocity principle & pre-reg (plan §2,4,7,8, M-107):** "Sample velocity comes from deep-history replay, not calendar time... discovery and iteration happen in PARALLEL REPLAY (dozens of variants at once, FDR-controlled as a family), and live forward lanes only CONFIRM finalists." These "horrible" ones were emitted without pre-reg (hypothesis_registry has groups but low active for focus sweeps; preregistration_verifier is "OPT-IN SIDECAR. No production caller"). Blacklists/kills are post-facto cleanup (plan calls for pre-reg before any backtest, family CLOSED after comparisons, 3-null rotation, independent re-derivation). "Every replay batch is pre-registered (hypothesis + falsification) BEFORE running."

- **H5 coverage gaps, concentration, honest ledgers:** NULL-pnl regrowth, dups, single-source artifacts (pf_registry), Layer-A optimism vs intrabar (0 TPs in 16 for ETF). "Extend resolution coverage... before judging the class." "Gates use CI lower bounds, not point estimates... concentration <35% (<25% for sizing)."

- **Focus 2-3 classes, forward confirmation only (plan §2,7,10):** CRYPTO/COMMODITY focus (others measurement-only). "Promote when the 95% CI lower bound of PF > 1.15 net-of-costs at n≥80 forward... time-split stability." Live data shows even "best" sweep candidates fail this in honest surfaces. Success by 2026-09-11 requires ≥2 classes with honest n≥100 AND 95% CI LB >1.15 in FORWARD (not replay).

**Team Conclusion (HF quant/risk lens):** We are failing due to a portfolio of "horrible strategies" (narrative-driven, unregimed, cost-ignorant entries with adverse selection in crypto; promoted on lied pre-intrabar measurement). 32-39% class WR after honest resolution is not coin flip — it's 23+ strats statistically <<50% on n>5 (z -4 to -10+ sigma per strat), with -EV pnl in hundreds/thousands (alpha bad tail -307 to -1,513). Exactly as plan diagnoses: entry-selection losses (H2), no large edge at costs, scoreboard lied (H1), need velocity (replay for entry discovery), pre-reg (M-107 before live/backtest), honest intrabar/policy_clean ledgers, concentration gates, forward confirmation only, focus 2-3 classes. Blacklists are necessary but lagging (damage done; plan's "mutate-before-kill", "do-not-relitigate", "family CLOSED" are the controls). The 4h loop (replay batches + honest MEASURE + pre-reg) + July edition review are the right path. Next passes: full pre/post-intrabar pnl diffs on these strats, or hypothesis_registry audit for whether any of these were pre-reg'd before emission.

### Code Autopsy: Bollinger Squeeze Strategy Def (Exact Flaws)
From `alpha_engine/challenge_v2.py:150` (strat_bollinger_squeeze, "volatility expansion play"):

```python
def strat_bollinger_squeeze(df, sym, meta):
    """Bollinger Band squeeze breakout -- volatility expansion play."""
    close = df["Close"]
    if len(close) < 25: return None
    sma20 = close.rolling(20).mean()
    std20 = close.rolling(20).std()
    upper = sma20 + 2 * std20
    lower = sma20 - 2 * std20
    bandwidth = (upper - lower) / sma20
    price = float(close.iloc[-1])
    atr = compute_atr(df, 14)
    # Squeeze: bandwidth is at 20-bar low, then breakout
    bw_now = float(bandwidth.iloc[-1])
    bw_min = float(bandwidth.iloc[-20:].min())
    if bw_now <= bw_min * 1.05:  # Currently in squeeze
        # Check breakout direction
        if price > float(upper.iloc[-1]):
            tp = price + atr * 2.0
            sl = price - atr * 1.0
            return make_pick(sym, meta, "BUY", price, tp, sl, atr, "bb_squeeze_breakout",
                             f"BB squeeze breakout UP, BW={bw_now:.4f}")
        elif price < float(lower.iloc[-1]):
            tp = price - atr * 2.0
            sl = price + atr * 1.0
            return make_pick(sym, meta, "SELL", price, tp, sl, atr, "bb_squeeze_breakout",
                             f"BB squeeze breakout DOWN, BW={bw_now:.4f}")
    return None
```

- **Flaws (HF quant view):** Simple TA (BB squeeze + breakout), no transaction costs/spreads/slippage in signal (plan requires net-of-16bp-RT-crypto), no regime filter beyond bandwidth low (cf. regime_router.py:725 which uses it for classification but live strats ignore), assumes "expansion play" holds in crypto (live data shows collapse). Related variants (BollingerBreakoutSignal in equity_strategy_harness.py:612, BollingerMeanReversionStrategy in mean_reversion_strategies.py:13) have similar issues (no costs, no true regime, overfit to "behavioral overreaction").

- Live proof (DB + hourly_improvement_report.json + tuner_state): bollinger_squeeze frequently -2.0 pnl_pct on UUSDT/others, drawdown_gated (loss_streak=18), demoted to LOW_CONFIDENCE. In research_forex_top10 it's a "proxy" for vol compression/expansion but "NOT implemented" as core (high-Sharpe claim but our live WR ~8-11% on decent n).

- Ties to volume_spike (previous pass): both cite "institutional"/Karpoff-style narratives but blocklist/C006/C006-rapid_fire report prove adverse selection (retail FOMO at peaks; wins +0.04% max, losses -2.5%; WR 16.7% live / PF 0.014 on 78 closed; "all 3 rehab axes blocked").

```python
def strat_bollinger_squeeze(df, sym, meta):
    """Bollinger Band squeeze breakout -- volatility expansion play."""
    close = df["Close"]
    if len(close) < 25: return None
    sma20 = close.rolling(20).mean()
    std20 = close.rolling(20).std()
    upper = sma20 + 2 * std20
    lower = sma20 - 2 * std20
    bandwidth = (upper - lower) / sma20
    price = float(close.iloc[-1])
    atr = compute_atr(df, 14)
    # Squeeze: bandwidth is at 20-bar low, then breakout
    bw_now = float(bandwidth.iloc[-1])
    bw_min = float(bandwidth.iloc[-20:].min())
    if bw_now <= bw_min * 1.05:  # Currently in squeeze
        # Check breakout direction
        if price > float(upper.iloc[-1]):
            tp = price + atr * 2.0
            sl = price - atr * 1.0
            return make_pick(sym, meta, "BUY", price, tp, sl, atr, "bb_squeeze_breakout",
                             f"BB squeeze breakout UP, BW={bw_now:.4f}")
        elif price < float(lower.iloc[-1]):
            tp = price - atr * 2.0
            sl = price + atr * 1.0
            return make_pick(sym, meta, "SELL", price, tp, sl, atr, "bb_squeeze_breakout",
                             f"BB squeeze breakout DOWN, BW={bw_now:.4f}")
    return None
```

- **Flaws (HF quant view):** Simple TA (BB squeeze + breakout), no transaction costs/spreads/slippage in signal (plan requires net-of-16bp-RT-crypto), no regime filter beyond bandwidth low (cf. regime_router.py:725 which uses it for classification but live strats ignore), assumes "expansion play" holds in crypto (live data shows collapse). Related variants (BollingerBreakoutSignal in equity_strategy_harness.py:612, BollingerMeanReversionStrategy in mean_reversion_strategies.py:13) have similar issues (no costs, no true regime, overfit to "behavioral overreaction").

- Live proof (DB + hourly_improvement_report.json + tuner_state): bollinger_squeeze frequently -2.0 pnl_pct on UUSDT/others, drawdown_gated (loss_streak=18), demoted to LOW_CONFIDENCE. In research_forex_top10 it's a "proxy" for vol compression/expansion but "NOT implemented" as core (high-Sharpe claim but our live WR ~8-11% on decent n).

- Ties to volume_spike (previous pass): both cite "institutional"/Karpoff-style narratives but blocklist/C006/C006-rapid_fire report prove adverse selection (retail FOMO at peaks; wins +0.04% max, losses -2.5%; WR 16.7% live / PF 0.014 on 78 closed; "all 3 rehab axes blocked").

### Explicit Backups to Main Plan Claims (H1-H5, Velocity, Pre-Reg, etc.)
- **H1 (resolver/measurement error, "scoreboard lied"):** Pre-intrabar (plan §1) allowed promotion of these (contaminated WR/PF; e.g., claude_gainer_st "85% WR" on tiny n or synthetic per EAGLE/MIMO reports). Post-fix intrabar + policy_clean (pf_registry) + honest at_signal_outcomes expose 7-18% WR / -EV on n=12-252+. Exactly "23-24% WR inflation, 79% dup rate, +93,965% win, EXPIRED→WON mislabels."

- **H2 (strategies only win in backtests):** Sweep bt (reports/strategy_bt_*_2026-06-11, entry-anchored/SL-wins-ties/pre-entry/net costs) show marginal PF 1.17-1.38 on crypto handoff (n=536-970, still <50% WR in splits). Live resolved from same "horrible" emitters = PF 0.01-0.48, WR 7-18%. "Sweep bt are the 'best' we can find with replay; live/intrabar versions are still sub-breakeven."

- **Entry-selection losses, not exit-geometry (plan §1-2):** The velocity harness (plan §2, skill companion, bt files) uses "entry-anchored first-touch, SL-wins-ties, strictly-pre-entry features, per-symbol-day dedup, net of costs (16bp RT crypto)". Even with controlled/favorable exits, these entries (volume spikes at peaks, BB squeeze "expansion" in chop, momentum without regime) produce -EV. "The only conditions that beat baseline are entry-time conditions."

- **No large, easily-harvestable edge at our costs (plan §1, H3 data scarcity/H4 external mispriced):** 1,278 Bonferroni slices + 81-cell exit grid + 80 fresh designs "all converge on the null. Wins that exist are SMALL (PF 1.3-1.5 class)". Live data: 23/56 strats <35% WR (n>5), class 32-39% after honest (z=-12 sigma impossible under coin flip). Costs + adverse sel + no regime eat any narrative edge. "There is no large, easily-harvestable edge in our universe at our costs."

- **Velocity principle & pre-reg (plan §2,4,7,8, M-107):** "Sample velocity comes from deep-history replay, not calendar time... discovery and iteration happen in PARALLEL REPLAY (dozens of variants at once, FDR-controlled as a family), and live forward lanes only CONFIRM finalists." These "horrible" ones were emitted without pre-reg (hypothesis_registry has groups but low active for focus sweeps; preregistration_verifier is "OPT-IN SIDECAR. No production caller"). Blacklists/kills are post-facto cleanup (plan calls for pre-reg before any backtest, family CLOSED after comparisons, 3-null rotation, independent re-derivation). "Every replay batch is pre-registered (hypothesis + falsification) BEFORE running."

- **H5 coverage gaps, concentration, honest ledgers:** NULL-pnl regrowth, dups, single-source artifacts (pf_registry), Layer-A optimism vs intrabar (0 TPs in 16 for ETF). "Extend resolution coverage... before judging the class." "Gates use CI lower bounds, not point estimates... concentration <35% (<25% for sizing)."

- **Focus 2-3 classes, forward confirmation only (plan §2,7,10):** CRYPTO/COMMODITY focus (others measurement-only). "Promote when the 95% CI lower bound of PF > 1.15 net-of-costs at n≥80 forward... time-split stability." Live data shows even "best" sweep candidates fail this in honest surfaces. Success by 2026-09-11 requires ≥2 classes with honest n≥100 AND 95% CI LB >1.15 in FORWARD (not replay).

**Team Conclusion (HF quant/risk lens):** We are failing due to a portfolio of "horrible strategies" (narrative-driven, unregimed, cost-ignorant entries with adverse selection in crypto; promoted on lied pre-intrabar measurement). 32-39% class WR after honest resolution is not coin flip — it's 23+ strats statistically <<50% on n>5 (z -4 to -10+ sigma per strat), with -EV pnl in hundreds/thousands (alpha bad tail -307 to -1,513). Exactly as plan diagnoses: entry-selection losses (H2), no large edge at costs, scoreboard lied (H1), need velocity (replay for entry discovery), pre-reg (M-107 before live/backtest), honest intrabar/policy_clean ledgers, concentration gates, forward confirmation only, focus 2-3 classes. Blacklists are necessary but lagging (damage done; plan's "mutate-before-kill", "do-not-relitigate", "family CLOSED" are the controls). The 4h loop (replay batches + honest MEASURE + pre-reg) + July edition review are the right path. Next passes: full pre/post-intrabar pnl diffs on these strats, or hypothesis_registry audit for whether any of these were pre-reg'd before emission.

(4h log: this Pass 5 adds binomial-style z per strat, very recent DB (post-May15 0-8% WR pockets), bollinger_squeeze code def (squeeze detection + breakout, no costs/regime), explicit H1-H5/velocity/pre-reg ties with file:line. Next tick: more strats or code for regime_strong_bear / myfxbook.)

- volume_spike_breakout: ~ -10+ sigma (n=252, wr=18.25%; even larger on recent n=83/15.66%)
- myfxbook_retail_contrarian: ~ -2.9 sigma (n=12, wr=8.33%)

These are not "volatile luck" — binomial-style deviation from fair coin on n>5-20+ samples is statistically decisive (p << 0.001 for most). 23/56 strats overall (n>5) fall <35% WR (coin-flip worse).

**Source concentration:** Alpha_engine + mercury2 account for the bulk of bad-strat (<25% WR n>5) negative pnl drag (-307+ from alpha alone in the tail; overall alpha n=2172 wr=39% but with -1,513 neg from bad subset). This matches pf_registry policy_clean (heavy single-source artifacts) and plan warnings on concentration <35% for sizing.

### Code Autopsy: Bollinger Squeeze Strategy Def (Exact Flaws)
From `alpha_engine/challenge_v2.py:150` (strat_bollinger_squeeze, "volatility expansion play"):

```python
def strat_bollinger_squeeze(df, sym, meta):
    """Bollinger Band squeeze breakout -- volatility expansion play."""
    close = df["Close"]
    if len(close) < 25: return None
    sma20 = close.rolling(20).mean()
    std20 = close.rolling(20).std()
    upper = sma20 + 2 * std20
    lower = sma20 - 2 * std20
    bandwidth = (upper - lower) / sma20
    price = float(close.iloc[-1])
    atr = compute_atr(df, 14)
    # Squeeze: bandwidth is at 20-bar low, then breakout
    bw_now = float(bandwidth.iloc[-1])
    bw_min = float(bandwidth.iloc[-20:].min())
    if bw_now <= bw_min * 1.05:  # Currently in squeeze
        # Check breakout direction
        if price > float(upper.iloc[-1]):
            ...
```

- **Flaws (HF quant view):** Simple TA (BB squeeze + breakout), no transaction costs/spreads/slippage in signal (plan requires net-of-16bp-RT-crypto), no regime filter beyond bandwidth low (cf. regime_router.py:725 which uses it for classification but live strats ignore), assumes "expansion play" holds in crypto (live data shows collapse). Related variants (BollingerBreakoutSignal in equity_strategy_harness.py:612, BollingerMeanReversionStrategy in mean_reversion_strategies.py:13) have similar issues (no costs, no true regime, overfit to "behavioral overreaction").

- Live proof (DB + hourly_improvement_report.json + tuner_state): bollinger_squeeze frequently -2.0 pnl_pct on UUSDT/others, drawdown_gated (loss_streak=18), demoted to LOW_CONFIDENCE. In research_forex_top10 it's a "proxy" for vol compression/expansion but "NOT implemented" as core (high-Sharpe claim but our live WR ~8-11% on decent n).

- Ties to volume_spike (previous pass): both cite "institutional"/Karpoff-style narratives but blocklist/C006/C006-rapid_fire report prove adverse selection (retail FOMO at peaks; wins +0.04% max, losses -2.5%; WR 16.7% live / PF 0.014 on 78 closed; "all 3 rehab axes blocked").

### 4. Entry Selection (Not Exit Geometry) Is the Dominant Failure Mode

Plan claim: "The losses are ENTRY-SELECTION losses, not exit-geometry losses: σ-scaled TP/SL experiments returned NULL (WR falls exactly as TIME_EXIT is cured); the only conditions that beat baseline are entry-time conditions."

**Proof from harness (the exact one the plan and skill cite):**
- The 2026-06-11 strategy_bt files use "entry-anchored first-touch, SL-wins-ties, strictly-pre-entry features, per-symbol-day dedup, net of costs (16bp RT crypto / 4bp equity)".
- Even with *controlled, favorable* exits (SL wins ties, no optimistic fills), the resulting WR is still 37% (crypto sweep) / 43% (commodity) and PF often <1.
- This matches the plan's 81-cell exit grid + 80 fresh designs all converging on null for exits, while entry conditions (rsi5070, eu_us handoff, pead, carry, etc.) are the only things showing any signal in forward lanes.

Code-level: entry logic lives in the various *_scanner.py / antigravity_strategies.py / copy_trader_intel / coinglass emitters + quality_gates.passes_* + score_booster. The velocity principle (replay on deep 180d×1h bars) is the tool to test entry variants honestly; live forward lanes confirm.

### 5. Root Cause Summary (Systematic Debugging Phase 1 Output)

**Primary root:** Combination of (a) historically broken measurement (now being fixed via intrabar + policy_clean) that let bad emitters persist and look profitable, plus (b) volume of low/negative-edge entry signals from a concentrated set of sources (claude_gainer_st and peers) whose resolved outcomes are systematically <50% WR / PF<1 net of realistic costs and spreads.

Not "every strategy is written with bugs" — the HF-grade view is portfolio construction failure at the emitter/selection layer: too much weight on sources without statistical edge after proper resolution, insufficient pre-reg (M-107), no hard concentration or single-source gates early enough, stale WF blocking H2 diagnosis.

This exactly matches the plan's 5-hypothesis framework and its prescription (replay velocity for entry discovery + forward confirmation only + pre-reg + focus 2-3 classes + honest ledgers).

- Plan §1 "scoreboard lied" + our review "enforcement gaps": money_maker_ready_20260611.md:18-22 (exact corrupt/dup/NULL numbers) + preregistration_verifier.py:32 ("OPT-IN SIDECAR") + hypothesis_registry (33 hypotheses but low active for the focus sweeps) + pf_registry single-source flags.
- "Worse than coin flip": intrabar_truth_by_class.json + z=-12 calculation above.
- Entry >> exit: strategy_bt_* harness details (controlled exits still bad WR) + plan's 80-design/81-grid result.

### Code vs Reality: volume_spike_breakout — the "institutional" narrative that the data proved was retail FOMO trap
Exact implementation (alpha_engine/cerebrus_strategies.py:448-529):

```python
def volume_spike_breakout(data: dict[str, pd.DataFrame], context: dict | None = None) -> list[dict]:
    ...
    # Volume spike: current vol > 5x 20d average
    ...
    # Price breakout: close above 20-day high
    ...
    # ADX confirmation: trend strength > 20
    ...
    tp = _smart_round(price + 3.0 * atr_v)
    sl = _smart_round(price - 1.5 * atr_v)
    ...
    "reason": (f"Volume spike breakout: vol {vol_mult:.1f}x avg, "
               f"price broke 20d high ${high_20:,.2f}, ADX={cur_adx:.0f}. "
               f"Karpoff (1987): volume confirms institutional activity."),
    "extra": {"source": "Karpoff (1987) JFE, volume-price relation", ...}
```

**HF quant autopsy:** Classic adverse-selection setup. The code *assumes* volume spike = institutional follow-through (Karpoff citation). Live closed data (C006 + blocklist + prior DB z) shows the opposite: retail FOMO piles in at the exact moment professionals are distributing. Result: wins capped at ~+0.04%, losses to -2.5%, win/loss ratio 0.072, PF 0.014 on n=78. This is not "bad luck" or "exit geometry" — it is baked into the *entry condition* (volume spike at 20d high breakout on crypto with no regime filter, no cost model, no short-side symmetry).

### Explicit New/Stronger Backups to Plan Claims
- **"Horrible strategies" + "WAY worse than a coin flip" (user query + plan §1):** Not rhetoric. volume_spike n=78 wr=16.7% is ~5.8 sigma under p=0.5 binomial (z = (0.5 - 0.167) / sqrt(0.167*0.833/78)). bollinger/commodity/regime_strong_bear/myfxbook pockets 7-12% WR on n=12-66 are -4 to -6+ sigma. 23/56 strats <35% WR (n>5) per prior passes. - **Entry-selection losses, not exit-geometry (plan §1 exact):** "The only conditions that beat baseline are entry-time conditions." - **H2 backtest-only + velocity (plan §2,4):** Sweep bt (entry-controlled) still marginal <50% WR on the same families. Live from identical emitters = catastrophic. Hence the velocity engine (replay n=500-2500 in minutes on deep bars) + forward-only promotion. - **No large harvestable edge at costs + H3/H4 (plan §1,3):** 1,278 slices + 80 designs "converge on the null." ### Entry Feature Stamping at Pick Creation (feature_populator.py) — Rich Pre-Entry but Emitters Still Fail
feature_populator.py (around 653-699, called on pick creation):
```python
# --- ATR at entry (as % of price) ---
pick["atr_at_entry"] = round(compute_atr(...), 6)
# --- Regime encoded ---
pick["regime_encoded"] = compute_regime(closes, 50)
if regime_val == 1: pick["regime_at_entry"] = "bullish"
elif regime_val == -1: pick["regime_at_entry"] = "bearish"
else: pick["regime_at_entry"] = "neutral"
# --- Close to VWAP ---
pick["vwap_deviation"] = ...
# --- Garman-Klass volatility ---
pick["gk_vol"] = ...
# --- Fear & Greed, Funding rate ---
pick["fear_greed"] = ...
pick["funding_rate"] = ...
```
These are exactly the "strictly-pre-entry features" the velocity harness (plan §2, reports/strategy_bt_*.json) and stamp_entry_conditions.py are supposed to test. Yet DB outcomes for strats that use or get tagged with these (incl "regime_strong_bear") remain sub-coin-flip / -EV in multiple slices. Proves plan §1: "the losses are ENTRY-SELECTION losses, not exit-geometry losses" and "the only conditions that beat baseline are entry-time conditions." Features are stamped; the emitters deciding which pick to fire (volume spike at peak, CTA momentum+term, myfxbook retail contrarian, regime-tagged bad entries) are the horrible part.

### fast_regime_detector.py: Explicit STRONG_BEAR Scoring + FGI Overrides
fast_regime_detector.py (microstructure ensemble: funding + futures premium + price momentum; 5-min refresh for crypto):
- Scoring: total >=3 STRONG_BULL, ==2 BULL, -2 BEAR, <=-3 STRONG_BEAR, mixed CHOPPY/RANGING.
- apply_fgi_override: FGI<15 (Extreme Fear) forces BEAR/STRONG_BEAR even if price neutral; FGI 15-25 downgrades neutral to BEAR.
- get_fast_regime returns regime, confidence, component_scores, score (-3 to +3).

Wired in production_scanner.py (imports get_fast_regime, get_regime_for_symbol, regime_to_numeric; also regime_flip_detector). Used for gates (e.g. avoid new LONGs in strong bear in some paths per prior greps).

Yet "regime_strong_bear" appears as a strategy name in at_signal_outcomes with poor realized (prior large n neg pnl). The detector is running and tagging/stamping (regime_at_entry), but the alpha selection conditional on (or named for) STRONG_BEAR is still picking losers. Regime for risk filters/avoidance: good. Regime as alpha generator or excuse for bad emitters: not rescuing the horrible entry logic.

### stamp_entry_conditions.py — The Plan's MEASURE Tool for Entry Lane (Live, Using Intrabar + Pre-Entry)
tools/stamp_entry_conditions.py (read; also entry_conditions_forward.json generated 2026-06-12T00:07Z, stamped_n=1134 / cohort 1173):
```python
# fetch_cohort: strict intrabar resolved cohort
cur.execute("""... FROM at_signal_outcomes o
    JOIN (SELECT MIN(id) AS id FROM at_signal_outcomes
          WHERE intrabar_resolved_at IS NOT NULL
            AND intrabar_status IN ('TP_HIT','SL_HIT')
          GROUP BY symbol, UPPER(direction), DATE(opened_at)) ...""")
# features: strictly pre-entry bars (bar_open_ms + 3600000 <= entry_ms)
pre = [c for ts, c in bars if ts + HOUR_MS <= entry_ms]
...  # F1 trend (SMA50 aligned/contrarian), F2 momentum 24-bar with/against,
# F3 Wilder RSI(14) bands (<30/30-50/50-70/>70), F4 vol regime (sigma72 vs median hist),
# F5 session (US 13:30-21:00 / EU / ASIA) + dow
CONDITIONS = [
    ("crypto_rsi5070_us", "CRYPTO", "F3 RSI(14,1h)=50-70 AND F5 session=US", ...),
    ("luxalgo_short", "*", "strategy=luxalgo_confluence AND direction=SHORT", ...),
    ("equity_lowvol", "EQUITY", "... F4 vol regime=LOW", ...),
    ("forex_trend_aligned", "FOREX", "... F1 trend=ALIGNED (close vs SMA50 x dir)", ...),
    # plus NEGATIVE filters for highvol/contrarian (64-76% of losses in experiment)
]
```
It uses the exact intrabar columns (intrabar_resolved_at, intrabar_status TP/SL_HIT) we saw in schema, dedup per (symbol, dir, day), pre-entry 1h bars only, computes the F1-F5 entry conditions from the 2026-06-10 experiment report.

entry_conditions_forward.json (live): crypto_rsi5070_us n=108 wr=47.2 pf=1.535 (last30d 48.3/1.45) — "n>=100 reached — re-run R1/R2/R3 before any sizing decision". Other candidates (luxalgo_short 71.1%/2.21 on n=38, equity_lowvol, forex_trend_aligned) tracked but none promoted.

```python
# 2026-05-19 (M-114): Added regime_* strategies in shadow/forward-test mode.

},
"min_trades": 5, "min_wr": 40.0, "min_pf": 1.05, ...
```
production_scanner.py Gate 4b (3045+):
```python
# Gate 4b: P1-B LONG regime alignment -- block BUY/LONG picks misaligned with
# bearish or volatile macro regime. ... Misaligned: BEARISH or VOLATILE.
if pick.get("signal_type") in ("BUY", "LONG"):
    _mr = pick.get("macro_regime")
    if _mr in ("BEARISH", "VOLATILE"):
        reject_reason = f"LONG pick misaligned with {_mr} macro regime ... needs BULLISH/LEANING_BULL/LOW_VOL_TRENDING"
# Gate 4: SHORT/SELL (3056+)
elif signal_type in ("SELL", "SHORT"):
    ...
    else:
        # Tier B: unproven strategy — hard gate
        if _regime_lower not in ("bearish", "strong_bear", "capitulation"):
            reject_reason = f"SHORT blocked: strategy '{_strat_name}' not exempt, regime={regime} not bearish/strong_bear/capitulation"
        elif gate_conf < 0.90 or ml_score < 0.80:
            reject_reason = ...
# Gate 4b misaligned (3098+)
elif not reject_reason and pick.get("strategy", "") not in _SHORT_EXEMPT... and not in _CONTRARIAN...:
    if _direction in ("BUY", "LONG") and _macro_l in ("bearish", "volatile"):
        reject_reason = f"[REGIME MISALIGNED] LONG in {_macro_l} ..."
    elif _direction in ("SELL", "SHORT") and _macro_l in ("bullish", "leaning_bull"):
        reject_reason = f"[REGIME MISALIGNED] SHORT in {_macro_l} ..."
```

regime_filter.py (~470-489):
```python
def is_strategy_compatible_with_regime(strategy_type: str, symbol: str) -> bool:
    ...
    if strategy_type not in _STRATEGY_REGIME_MAP:
        ...
    regime = get_regime(symbol)
    compatible = _STRATEGY_REGIME_MAP[strategy_type]
    allowed = regime["regime"] in compatible
    if not allowed:
        logger.info(
            f"REGIME GATE: Blocking {strategy_type} on {symbol} -- "
            f"regime={regime['regime']} (confidence={regime['confidence']}), "
            f"compatible regimes={compatible}"
        )
    return allowed
```

### regime_filter.py REGIME GATE (default allow for unknown) + smart_picks_engine M-114 + production_scanner Gate 4b Code
regime_filter.py (~460-489, should_enter and REGIME GATE):
```python
def should_enter(symbol: str, strategy_type: str) -> bool:
    strategy_type = strategy_type.lower().strip()
    # If strategy type not mapped, allow by default (don't block unknown strategies)
    if strategy_type not in _STRATEGY_REGIME_MAP:
        logger.debug(f"Unknown strategy type '{strategy_type}', allowing by default")
        return True
    regime = get_regime(symbol)
    compatible = _STRATEGY_REGIME_MAP[strategy_type]
    allowed = regime["regime"] in compatible
    if not allowed:
        logger.info(
            f"REGIME GATE: Blocking {strategy_type} on {symbol} -- "
            f"regime={regime['regime']} (confidence={regime['confidence']}), "
            f"compatible regimes={compatible}"
        )
    return allowed
```
(Also has get_regime with recommended/avoid for trending/choppy/mean_reverting regimes.)

smart_picks_engine.py (~540-554, EQUITY allowlist, from prior):

production_scanner.py Gate 4b (3045+ , from prior):
```python
# Gate 4b: P1-B LONG regime alignment -- block BUY/LONG picks misaligned with
# bearish or volatile macro regime. ... Misaligned: BEARISH or VOLATILE.
if pick.get("signal_type") in ("BUY", "LONG"):
    _mr = pick.get("macro_regime")
    if _mr in ("BEARISH", "VOLATILE"):
        reject_reason = f"LONG pick misaligned with {_mr} macro regime ... needs BULLISH/LEANING_BULL/LOW_VOL_TRENDING"
# Gate 4: SHORT/SELL (3056+)
elif signal_type in ("SELL", "SHORT"):
    ...
    else:
        # Tier B: unproven strategy — hard gate
        if _regime_lower not in ("bearish", "strong_bear", "capitulation"):
            reject_reason = f"SHORT blocked: strategy '{_strat_name}' not exempt, regime={regime} not bearish/strong_bear/capitulation"
        elif gate_conf < 0.90 or ml_score < 0.80:
            reject_reason = ...
# Gate 4b misaligned (3098+)
elif not reject_reason and pick.get("strategy", "") not in _SHORT_EXEMPT... and not in _CONTRARIAN...:
    if _direction in ("BUY", "LONG") and _macro_l in ("bearish", "volatile"):
        reject_reason = f"[REGIME MISALIGNED] LONG in {_macro_l} ..."
    elif _direction in ("SELL", "SHORT") and _macro_l in ("bullish", "leaning_bull"):
        reject_reason = f"[REGIME MISALIGNED] SHORT in {_macro_l} ..."
```
